````html
        </section>
        <div class="education-list">
          <article class="entry">
            <h3><span>Licensed Nurse Practitioner</span><span>Year of 2010-2013</span></h3>
            <p>Completed clinical training in patient care,<br>ward assessment and safe daily practice,<br>with supervised placements in local clinics.</p>
          </article>
          <article class="entry">
            <h3><span>RN Bachelor</span><span>Year of 2013-2015</span></h3>
            <p>Studied health science and nursing methods,<br>with a focus on patient teaching and careful<br>planning for community care.</p>
          </article>
          <article class="entry">
            <h3><span>Neonatal MS</span><span>Year of 2015-2017</span></h3>
            <p>Advanced study in infant care and recovery,<br>with guided practice in family support and<br>evidence-led clinical decisions.</p>
          </article>
        </div>
      </div>
    </section>
    <section class="experience-region" aria-label="Professional experience">

          <h3><span>North Bay Hospital</span><span>2017-2018</span></h3>
          <p>Supported routine rounds and patient intake,<br>sharing clear updates with the nursing team<br>and maintaining accurate care records.</p>
        </article>
        <article class="entry">
          <h3><span>Willow Children's Center</span><span>2018-2019</span></h3>
          <p>Assisted with pediatric care and family visits,<br>helping young patients feel informed and safe<br>through steady, compassionate support.</p>
        </article>
        <article class="entry">
          <h3><span>Harborview Hospital</span><span>2019-Present</span></h3>
          <p>Coordinate daily care across a busy ward,<br>reviewing patient progress with clinical teams<br>and supporting safe discharge planning.</p>
        </article>
        <article class="entry">
          <h3><span>Meadow Care Hospital</span><span>2021-Present</span></h3>
          <p>Provide flexible support for outreach clinics,<br>offering health guidance and follow-up care<br>with a focus on lasting patient confidence.</p>
        </article>
      </div>
    </section>

        </ul>
````

## 简历-字体潮流-蓝色散字侧轨与局部方格-高

逻辑路径：assets/模板库/构图/简历-字体潮流-蓝色散字侧轨与局部方格-高.html

````html
<!doctype html>
<html lang="en"><head><meta name="standalone-template" content="1"><style data-standalone-base>html,body{margin:0;padding:0}</style><style data-compiled-dependencies>.vl-grid { display:grid; grid-template-columns:repeat(var(--vl-grid-columns,2),minmax(0,1fr)); gap:var(--vl-grid-gap,16px); align-items:var(--vl-grid-align,stretch); }
.vl-grid > * { min-width:0; overflow-wrap:anywhere; }
:where(.vl-grid img) { display:block; width:100%; height:auto; }
.vl-grid--ratio { grid-template-columns:var(--vl-grid-ratio,1fr 1fr); }

.cvx-stacked-label-track,.cvx-stacked-label-track *{box-sizing:border-box;min-width:0;max-width:100%}
.cvx-stacked-label-track{font-synthesis:none;overflow-wrap:anywhere}
.cvx-stacked-label-track h2{margin:0 0 var(--cvx-heading-gap,.75rem)}
.cvx-track-note{margin:0 0 var(--cvx-track-row-gap,1rem)}
.cvx-track-list{display:grid;grid-template-columns:minmax(0,1fr);gap:var(--cvx-track-row-gap,1rem);margin:0;padding:0;list-style:none}
.cvx-track-item{display:grid;grid-template-columns:minmax(0,1fr);row-gap:var(--cvx-label-track-gap,.4rem)}
.cvx-track-label{display:block;white-space:normal}
.cvx-thin-track{block-size:var(--cvx-track-thickness,2px);background:var(--cvx-track-rest,color-mix(in srgb,currentColor 25%,transparent))}
.cvx-thin-track-fill{display:block;inline-size:clamp(0%,calc(var(--cvx-value,0)*1%),100%);block-size:100%;background:var(--cvx-track-ink,currentColor)}

.cvx-scattered-letter-rail,.cvx-scattered-letter-rail *{box-sizing:border-box;min-width:0;max-width:100%}
.cvx-scattered-letter-rail{display:grid;grid-template-columns:minmax(0,min(var(--cvx-rail-width,6rem),30%)) minmax(0,1fr);column-gap:min(var(--cvx-rail-gap,1.25rem),5%);font-synthesis:none;overflow-wrap:anywhere}
.cvx-scattered-rail{position:relative;overflow:hidden;align-self:stretch;color:var(--cvx-rail-ink,currentColor);pointer-events:none;user-select:none}
.cvx-scattered-letter{position:absolute;display:block;max-width:none;left:var(--cvx-letter-x,0%);top:calc(var(--cvx-letter-index,0)*var(--cvx-rail-step,4.5rem) + var(--cvx-letter-y,0rem));font-size:var(--cvx-letter-size,6rem);font-weight:700;line-height:.9;transform:rotate(var(--cvx-letter-angle,0deg));transform-origin:center}
.cvx-scattered-content{display:grid;align-content:start;gap:var(--cvx-content-gap,1.25rem)}
.cvx-scattered-content h2,.cvx-scattered-content h3,.cvx-scattered-content p{margin:0}
.cvx-scattered-content header,.cvx-scattered-content section{display:grid;gap:var(--cvx-paragraph-gap,.5rem)}
.cvx-scattered-content address{font-style:normal}
.cvx-scattered-content a{color:inherit}
.cvx-scattered-content ul{margin:0;padding-inline-start:1.25em}

          <p class="cvx-sue-copy" data-text="sue-work-one-description">Designed printed journals and visual identities for independent cultural projects. Worked with editors and illustrators to bring clear typography, original imagery and a consistent voice to every publication.</p>

        <article class="cvx-sue-record" data-safe-block="sue-work-little-field">
          <h3 data-text="sue-work-two-title">Illustrator · Little Field Studio</h3>

        </article>
````

````html
<div class="about-copy"><p>专注数字产品设计与用户研究，实习期间参与线上服务平台改版，通过A/B测试优化注册路径并提升转化率；熟悉界面规范与交互原型，独立完成校园文创项目及品牌视觉方案，具备快速学习与逻辑思维，获得青年设计展入选认可。</p><p>职业目标: 以扎实的技术能力与用户洞察力，助力企业打造高价值产品体验。</p></div>
</section>
<section class="work-section" aria-labelledby="resume-01-work">

<div class="work-list">
<article class="work-item"><div class="item-top"><h3>星序创意</h3><span class="date">2023-2024</span></div><p class="position">视觉设计助理</p><p class="work-description">在星序创意担任视觉设计助理，协助品牌完成季度传播策划，梳理企业宣传物料及效果，提升品牌识别度。在项目中参与海报与活动页面设计，整理组件规范并配合团队完成多端视觉交付，跟进不同渠道的尺寸适配与输出检查，归档版本反馈及交付文件。</p></article>
<article class="work-item"><div class="item-top"><h3>青屿科技</h3><span class="date">2022-2023</span></div><p class="position">产品设计助理</p><p class="work-description">在青屿科技担任产品设计助理，参与线上服务页面与用户流程优化，整理调研反馈及竞品资料，完善界面信息层级。在版本迭代中协同产品与开发同事，完成原型评审和上线视觉验收，补充关键页面的交互状态说明，记录测试问题并跟进修订结果。</p></article>
<article class="work-item"><div class="item-top"><h3>远岚工作室</h3><span class="date">2021-2022</span></div><p class="position">品牌设计实习</p><p class="work-description">在远岚工作室参与品牌设计实习，协助完成文化项目视觉提案，延展宣传海报及展陈物料，提升活动传播效果。在团队协作中整理图形资产与排版规范，支持项目提案展示及制作落地，核对印刷文件的色彩与出血设置，协助整理样稿及制作反馈记录。</p></article>
</div>

<article class="award-item"><div class="item-top"><h3>青屿数字体验设计赛</h3><span class="date">2023</span></div><p class="award-description">参与2023年青屿数字体验设计赛获得交互专项奖，并以校园服务改版方案获评年度优秀作品展示资格</p></article>
````

````html
<div class="entry-head"><h3>DESIGN DIPLOMA</h3><span class="date">2014-2016</span></div>
<p>Studied visual form at Northbank Arts, exploring typography, image and editorial systems. Built clear layouts for print projects and small community exhibitions.</p>

<article class="entry">
<div class="entry-head"><h3>BA VISUAL DESIGN</h3><span class="date">2016-2018</span></div>
<p>Developed identity projects at Westmere College with a focus on research, structure and careful production. Final work explored accessible design for local cultural spaces.</p>

<p>Created print materials for Fieldline Studio, turning early sketches into practical layouts. Prepared artwork, checked proofs and supported a small team across daily deadlines.</p>
</article>
<article class="entry">
<div class="entry-head"><h3>BRAND DESIGNER</h3><span class="date">2020-2022</span></div>
<p>Shaped visual identities at Common North, designing marks, guides and campaign assets. Worked with writers and account teams to keep each launch consistent and clear.</p>
</article>
<article class="entry">
<div class="entry-head"><h3>SENIOR DESIGNER</h3><span class="date">2022-2026</span></div>
````

````html
main.cvX-montminy[data-resume-page="a4"]{--cvx-pink-ink:#943b80;--cvx-profession-ink:#4c286b}
main.cvX-montminy .cvx-education h3,main.cvX-montminy .cvx-experience h3,main.cvX-montminy .cvx-job-meta{color:var(--cvx-pink-ink)}

main.cvX-montminy .cvx-family-name{color:var(--cvx-profession-ink)}
</style></head><body><main data-resume-page="a4" data-safe-inset="48" data-density="normal" data-content-status="fictional-layout-sample-replace-all-personal-data" class="cvx-montminy cvX-montminy" data-recipe="combination.resume-pink-name-collage" data-safe-block="resume" lang="en" aria-label="Amora Montrose résumé template">

  <div class="cvx-content">
    <section class="cvx-about" data-safe-block="about" aria-labelledby="cvx-mm-about">
      <div class="cvx-heading-row"><h2 class="cvx-heading" id="cvx-mm-about" data-text="about-heading">about me</h2><div class="cvx-dotted-rule" aria-hidden="true"></div></div>
      <p data-text="about-copy">I've been working in digital marketing for several years, with experience in both strategic planning and day-to-day execution. I've managed campaigns, worked with content, and tracked performance to help increase visibility and reach. I believe digital marketing is about creating meaningful exposure and connection.</p>
    </section>
    <div class="cvx-columns vl-grid vl-grid--ratio" data-css-atom="layout.evidence-grid">
      <div class="cvx-left-column">
        <section class="cvx-education" data-safe-block="education" aria-labelledby="cvx-mm-education">

          <div class="cvx-education-list">
            <article data-safe-block="education-degree"><h3 data-text="degree-title">Bachelor's Degree in Marketing &amp; Business</h3><p data-text="degree-details">Thynk University / <time datetime="2016">2016</time> - <time datetime="2020">2020</time></p></article>
            <article data-safe-block="education-certificate"><h3 data-text="certificate-title">Professional Certification in Digital Marketing</h3><p data-text="certificate-details">Digital Marketing Solution / <time datetime="2021">2021</time></p></article>
          </div>
        </section>
        <section class="cvx-skills" data-safe-block="skills" aria-labelledby="cvx-mm-skills">
          <h2 class="cvx-heading" id="cvx-mm-skills" data-text="skills-heading">skills</h2>
          <ul class="cvx-skill-list vl-grid" data-css-atom="layout.evidence-grid">
            <li><span class="vl-badge cvx-skill-badge" data-css-atom="shape.badge" data-text="skill-strategy">Online<br>campaign<br>strategy</span></li>
            <li><span class="vl-badge cvx-skill-badge" data-css-atom="shape.badge" data-text="skill-seo">SEO and basic<br>analytics</span></li>
            <li><span class="vl-badge cvx-skill-badge" data-css-atom="shape.badge" data-text="skill-media">Paid media<br>planning</span></li>
            <li><span class="vl-badge cvx-skill-badge" data-css-atom="shape.badge" data-text="skill-brand">Brand exposure<br>techniques</span></li>
          </ul>
        </section>
      </div>
      <section class="cvx-experience" data-safe-block="experience" aria-labelledby="cvx-mm-experience">
        <h2 class="cvx-heading" id="cvx-mm-experience" data-text="experience-heading">experience</h2>
        <div class="cvx-experience-list">
          <article data-safe-block="experience-assistant">
            <h3 data-text="assistant-title">Marketing Assistant</h3>
            <p class="cvx-job-meta" data-text="assistant-meta">Borcelle / <time datetime="2021">2021</time> - <time datetime="2022">2022</time></p>
            <ul class="cvx-job-points"><li data-text="assistant-task-1">Assisted with digital campaign setup and data tracking.</li><li data-text="assistant-task-2">Created basic ads and newsletters.</li></ul>
          </article>
          <article data-safe-block="experience-executive">
            <h3 data-text="executive-title">Digital Marketing Executive</h3>
            <p class="cvx-job-meta" data-text="executive-meta">Borcelle / <time datetime="2022">2022</time> - <time datetime="2023">2023</time></p>
            <ul class="cvx-job-points"><li data-text="executive-task-1">Planned and executed PPC, SEO, and email campaigns.</li><li data-text="executive-task-2">Monitored analytics dashboards and generated performance reports.</li><li data-text="executive-task-3">Coordinated with design and content teams.</li></ul>
          </article>
          <article data-safe-block="experience-specialist">
            <h3 data-text="specialist-title">Digital Marketing Specialist</h3>
            <p class="cvx-job-meta" data-text="specialist-meta">Larana / <time datetime="2023">2023</time> - Present</p>
            <ul class="cvx-job-points"><li data-text="specialist-task-1">Managed end-to-end digital strategy across multiple channels.</li><li data-text="specialist-task-2">Optimized campaigns based on performance data.</li><li data-text="specialist-task-3">Increased ROI through targeted digital efforts.</li></ul>
          </article>
        </div>
        <div class="cvx-experience-rule" aria-hidden="true"></div>
      </section>
    </div>
  </div>
  <footer class="cvx-footer" data-safe-block="footer">
    <address class="cvx-contact-list" data-safe-block="contact">
      <div class="cvx-contact-item"><svg class="cvx-contact-icon" viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="10.5"/><rect x="6" y="8" width="12" height="8" rx="1"/><path d="m6 8 6 5 6-5"/></svg><p data-text="contact-email"><a href="mailto:hello@example.com">hello@example.com</a></p></div>
      <div class="cvx-contact-item"><svg class="cvx-contact-icon" viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="10.5"/><path d="M12 18s5-5.2 5-8a5 5 0 0 0-10 0c0 2.8 5 8 5 8Z"/><circle cx="12" cy="10" r="1.5"/></svg><p data-text="contact-address">123 Anywhere St., Any City, ST 12345</p></div>
      <div class="cvx-contact-item"><svg class="cvx-contact-icon" viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="10.5"/><path d="m8 6-2 2c0 5 5 10 10 10l2-2-3-3-2 1c-2-1-3-2-3-3l1-2Z"/></svg><p data-text="contact-phone"><a href="tel:+1234567890">+123-456-7890</a></p></div>
      <div class="cvx-contact-item"><svg class="cvx-contact-icon" viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="10.5"/><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="7"/><ellipse cx="12" cy="12" rx="3" ry="7"/><path d="M5 12h14M6 8h12M6 16h12"/></svg><p data-text="contact-social">@example</p></div>
    </address>
  </footer>
</main></body></html>
````

````html
<style data-resume-css="09">#resume-09{color:rgb(17, 17, 17);background-color:rgb(65, 154, 197);}

#resume-09 .identity{background-image:linear-gradient(118deg, rgb(251, 251, 248), rgb(246, 246, 241));background-color:initial;box-shadow:var(--resume-surface,rgba(52, 78, 86, 0.125)) 0px 1px 2px;}

#resume-09 .envelope{background-image:initial;background-color:var(--resume-surface,rgb(232, 218, 206));border-top-color:var(--resume-muted,rgb(214, 196, 182));border-right-color:var(--resume-muted,rgb(214, 196, 182));border-bottom-color:var(--resume-muted,rgb(214, 196, 182));border-left-color:var(--resume-muted,rgb(214, 196, 182));border-image-source:none;border-image-slice:100%;border-image-width:1;border-image-outset:0;border-image-repeat:stretch;}
#resume-09 .envelope::before{background-image:linear-gradient(32deg, transparent 48%, rgb(203, 183, 166) 49%, transparent 51%), linear-gradient(-32deg, transparent 48%, rgb(203, 183, 166) 49%, transparent 51%);background-color:initial;}

#resume-09 .education{background-image:linear-gradient(110deg, rgb(245, 242, 237), rgb(238, 235, 230));background-color:initial;box-shadow:var(--resume-ink,rgba(41, 63, 75, 0.09)) 2px 2px 3px;}
#resume-09 .education::after{background-image:initial;background-color:var(--resume-paper,rgb(241, 238, 233));}

#resume-09 .edu-item.last{border-top-color:currentcolor;border-right-color:currentcolor;border-bottom-color:currentcolor;border-left-color:currentcolor;border-image-source:none;border-image-slice:100%;border-image-width:1;border-image-outset:0;border-image-repeat:stretch;}

#resume-09 .skills::before{opacity:0.18;background-image:repeating-linear-gradient(0deg, transparent 0px, transparent 2px, rgba(185, 183, 170, 0.086) 2px, rgba(185, 183, 170, 0.086) 3px), url("data:image/svg+xml;base64,PHN2ZyB4bWxucz0iaHR0cDovL3d3dy53My5vcmcvMjAwMC9zdmciIHdpZHRoPSIxNTAiIGhlaWdodD0iMTUwIj48ZmlsdGVyIGlkPSJnIj48ZmVUdXJidWxlbmNlIGJhc2VGcmVxdWVuY3k9Ii43IiBudW1PY3RhdmVzPSIzIiBzdGl0Y2hUaWxlcz0ic3RpdGNoIi8+PC9maWx0ZXI+PHBhdGggZmlsdGVyPSJ1cmwoI2cpIiBvcGFjaXR5PSIuNCIgZD0iTTAgMGgxNTB2MTUwSDB6Ii8+PC9zdmc+");}
#resume-09 .app{background-image:initial;background-color:var(--resume-paper,rgb(220, 237, 248));color:var(--resume-ink,rgb(23, 28, 36));font-family:var(--resume-body-font,Arial, sans-serif);box-shadow:var(--resume-ink,rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.467)) 0px 1px 1px;}
#resume-09 .paper-tape{filter:drop-shadow(rgba(73, 55, 25, 0.23) 1px 2px 1px);}
#resume-09 .paper-tape::before{background-image:linear-gradient(167deg, rgb(207, 184, 121) 0px, rgb(207, 184, 121) 37%, rgb(169, 138, 70) 38%, rgb(169, 138, 70) 41%, rgb(196, 168, 100) 43%, rgb(196, 168, 100) 100%);background-color:initial;}
#resume-09 .paper-tape::after{background-image:linear-gradient(13deg, rgb(178, 148, 82), rgb(213, 192, 137));background-color:initial;border-top-color:var(--resume-accent,rgb(233, 219, 166));}
#resume-09 .clear-tape{background-image:linear-gradient(173deg, rgba(218, 229, 231, 0.04) 0px, rgba(218, 229, 231, 0.04) 27%, rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.314) 29%, rgba(154, 173, 185, 0.137) 34%, rgba(220, 229, 230, 0.17) 49%, rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.463) 52%, rgba(116, 133, 145, 0.07) 55%, rgba(232, 237, 237, 0.22) 100%);background-color:initial;border-top-color:var(--resume-paper,rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.224));border-right-color:var(--resume-paper,rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.224));border-bottom-color:var(--resume-paper,rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.224));border-left-color:var(--resume-paper,rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.224));border-image-source:none;border-image-slice:100%;border-image-width:1;border-image-outset:0;border-image-repeat:stretch;}
#resume-09 .clear-tape::before{background-image:linear-gradient(86deg, transparent 14%, rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.29) 15%, transparent 16%, transparent 30%, rgba(226, 231, 233, 0.333) 31%, transparent 32%, transparent 77%, rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.333) 78%, transparent 79%);background-color:initial;}
#resume-09 .clear-tape::after{background-image:initial;background-color:var(--resume-paper,rgba(247, 250, 249, 0.63));box-shadow:var(--resume-paper,rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.145)) 23px -8px 0px, var(--resume-paper,rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.208)) -17px 4px 0px;}
````

````html
<div class="about-copy"><p>专注数字产品设计与用户研究，实习期间参与线上服务平台改版，通过A/B测试优化注册路径并提升转化率；熟悉界面规范与交互原型，独立完成校园文创项目及品牌视觉方案，具备快速学习与逻辑思维，获得青年设计展入选认可。</p><p>职业目标: 以扎实的技术能力与用户洞察力，助力企业打造高价值产品体验。</p></div>
</section>
<section class="work-section" aria-labelledby="resume-01-work">
<header class="masthead"><h2 id="resume-01-work">Work</h2><span class="zh-title">工作经历</span></header>
<div class="work-list">
<article class="work-item"><div class="item-top"><h3>星序创意</h3><span class="date">2023-2024</span></div><p class="position">视觉设计助理</p><p class="work-description">在星序创意担任视觉设计助理，协助品牌完成季度传播策划，梳理企业宣传物料及效果，提升品牌识别度。在项目中参与海报与活动页面设计，整理组件规范并配合团队完成多端视觉交付，跟进不同渠道的尺寸适配与输出检查，归档版本反馈及交付文件。</p></article>
<article class="work-item"><div class="item-top"><h3>青屿科技</h3><span class="date">2022-2023</span></div><p class="position">产品设计助理</p><p class="work-description">在青屿科技担任产品设计助理，参与线上服务页面与用户流程优化，整理调研反馈及竞品资料，完善界面信息层级。在版本迭代中协同产品与开发同事，完成原型评审和上线视觉验收，补充关键页面的交互状态说明，记录测试问题并跟进修订结果。</p></article>
<article class="work-item"><div class="item-top"><h3>远岚工作室</h3><span class="date">2021-2022</span></div><p class="position">品牌设计实习</p><p class="work-description">在远岚工作室参与品牌设计实习，协助完成文化项目视觉提案，延展宣传海报及展陈物料，提升活动传播效果。在团队协作中整理图形资产与排版规范，支持项目提案展示及制作落地，核对印刷文件的色彩与出血设置，协助整理样稿及制作反馈记录。</p></article>
</div>
</section>
<section class="awards-section" aria-labelledby="resume-01-awards">

<div class="award-list">
<article class="award-item"><div class="item-top"><h3>星河青年创意设计赛</h3><span class="date">2024</span></div><p class="award-description">参与2024年星河青年创意设计赛获得入围优秀奖，并以城市文化视觉提案获评年度新锐作品展示资格</p></article>
<article class="award-item"><div class="item-top"><h3>青屿数字体验设计赛</h3><span class="date">2023</span></div><p class="award-description">参与2023年青屿数字体验设计赛获得交互专项奖，并以校园服务改版方案获评年度优秀作品展示资格</p></article>
<article class="award-item"><div class="item-top"><h3>远山校园视觉设计赛</h3><span class="date">2022</span></div><p class="award-description">参与2022年远山校园视觉设计赛获得品牌创意奖，并以公共阅读视觉方案获评年度优秀作品展示资格</p></article>
</div>
</section>

</main></body></html>
````
